Motivation: 现有离散扩散模型的可控生成方法要么计算开销大，要么需要重新训练，缺乏高效的即插即用方案。

Method: 提出GILC框架，利用预训练去噪网络作为变分代理来估计引导信号；引入无雅可比机制直接校正干净预测logits，避免高维离散空间中的梯度不稳定问题；支持可微和不可微奖励函数。

Result: 在DNA、蛋白质序列和分子生成任务上达到SOTA性能，且无需额外训练，经常优于微调方法。

Conclusion: GILC是一种高效、即插即用的离散扩散模型可控生成方法，无需重新训练即可在各种任务上超越现有方法。

Abstract: Controllable generation with discrete diffusion models is often hindered by high computational overhead or the need for retraining. In this paper, we present \underline{\textbf{G}}radient-\underline{\textbf{I}}nformed \underline{\textbf{L}}ogit \underline{\textbf{C}}orrection (\textbf{GILC}), a plug-and-play framework that efficiently estimates guidance signals by repurposing the pretrained denoising network as a variational proxy. To circumvent the gradient instability inherent in high-dimensional discrete spaces, we introduce a Jacobian-free mechanism that directly corrects the clean prediction logits, facilitating stable and effective guidance. Our method accommodates both differentiable and non-differentiable reward functions. Extensive experiments across DNA, protein sequence, and molecular generation tasks demonstrate that GILC achieves state-of-the-art performance without additional training, frequently outperforming fine-tuning approaches.

</details>


### [104] [Learning What to Forget: Improving LLM Unlearning via Learned Token-Level Importance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06320)
*Gizem Yüce,Giorgos Nikolaou,Nicolas Flammarion*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ATWU框架，通过保留冲突准则联合学习令牌权重和模型参数，在不依赖外部标注的情况下实现自回归语言模型的高效细粒度机器遗忘，在TOFU和RWKU上取得最佳遗忘-保留权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器遗忘方法忽略了遗忘样本中不同令牌对遗忘目标的相关性差异，要么将其等同对待，要么依赖辅助模型、启发式规则或外部标注来估计令牌相关性，缺乏一个原则性的、自监督的令牌级遗忘重要性度量标准。

Method: 提出交替令牌加权遗忘（ATWU）框架：将遗忘过程形式化为模型参数和令牌权重的联合优化问题，基于"保留冲突"准则——令牌的遗忘专用性取决于最小化该令牌遗忘损失与保留最优性之间的冲突程度；使用轻量级线性打分器基于隐藏状态预测令牌权重，并与模型参数交替优化。

Result: 在TOFU和RWKU基准上，ATWU在遗忘-保留权衡上达到最优，优于样本级方法、基于概率的令牌加权启发式方法以及基于辅助模型的方法；学习到的令牌分数与真实遗忘相关跨度高度对齐，表明能识别语义有意义的令牌级遗忘信号。

Conclusion: 保留冲突是识别语言模型应遗忘内容的有效准则，能够直接从模型表示中无监督学习令牌级遗忘专用性，且计算开销极低。

Abstract: Machine unlearning aims to remove targeted knowledge from a trained model while preserving its general capabilities. For autoregressive language models, not all tokens in a forget sample are equally relevant to forgetting. Existing approaches either ignore this heterogeneity or rely on auxiliary models, heuristics, or external annotations to estimate each token's relevance for forgetting. We instead characterize it through the interaction with the retain objective: a token is forget-specific to the extent that minimizing the forget loss on that token does not conflict with retain optimality. We formalize this perspective as a joint optimization problem over the model parameters and the token weights and show that, under a natural separation condition, the resulting objective recovers the oracle forget-specific token support. Motivated by this formulation, we introduce Alternating Token-Weighted Unlearning (ATWU), a lightweight framework that jointly learns token forget-specificity and model parameters during unlearning using a simple linear scorer over the hidden states, without external token level supervision. Across TOFU and RWKU, ATWU achieves state of the art forget-retain trade-offs, outperforming sample-level methods, probability-based token weighting heuristics, and auxiliary-model-based approaches. Moreover, the learned scores align substantially better with ground truth forget-specific spans, indicating that ATWU identifies semantically meaningful token level forgetting signals. Overall, our results suggest that retain conflict provides an effective criterion for identifying what language models should forget, enabling unsupervised learning of token level forget-specificity directly from model representations with minimal computational overhead.

</details>


### [105] [PAMF: Prior-Aware Multimodal Fusion for Incomplete Time Series Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06328)
*Ziwen Kan,Wugeng Zheng,Tianlong Chen,Song Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PAMF框架，通过先验感知流匹配和权重共享，显式处理多模态时间序列中两种缺失模式（模态内缺失和模态级缺失），并将插补与下游预测任务耦合，在多个医疗基准上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常通过掩码或缺失嵌入隐式表示未观测数据，缺乏实例级缺失信息学习，且多数仅针对一种缺失模式设计；而现有插补方法对不同结构性先验的缺失模式统一处理，且插补过程与下游任务隔离，无法引导插补生成更具信息量的表示。

Method: PAMF框架包含两个核心设计：1) 利用类型特异性先验初始化流匹配的源状态，区分两种缺失类型；2) 通过架构匹配的编码器与权重共享，将插补与分类任务连接，将任务相关表示传递到插补过程。

Result: 在多个多模态医疗时间序列基准上，PAMF在不同数据集和缺失场景下均取得了最强的整体下游性能。

Conclusion: 显式建模不同缺失模式的结构性先验，并将插补与下游任务耦合，能有效提升多模态时间序列在不完全观测下的下游任务性能。

Abstract: In healthcare, multimodal time series tasks often operate on incomplete observations in practice, for example when ECG segments are lost because electrodes detach or an entire respiratory channel is unavailable during overnight monitoring. Such missingness typically appears in two structurally distinct patterns: within-modality missing, where values are absent within an otherwise observed modality, and modality-level missing, where an entire modality is unavailable. Existing methods typically represent unobserved data implicitly through masks or missing embeddings, without learning instance-specific missing information, and most are designed for only one missingness pattern. A natural approach is to explicitly estimate the missing data; however, existing imputation methods treat missingness uniformly despite their different structural priors, and the imputation process is often isolated from downstream tasks, preventing downstream tasks from guiding imputation toward more informative representations. To address these limitations, we present PAMF, a multimodal time-series framework that explicitly handles different missingness patterns while coupling imputation with downstream prediction through prior-aware flow matching and weight sharing. Specifically, the method initializes the flow-matching source state with type-specific priors to distinguish two missing types. It further connects imputation and classification through architecturally matched encoders with weight sharing, transferring task-relevant representations into the imputation process. Experiments on multiple multimodal healthcare time-series benchmarks show that the proposed method achieves the strongest overall downstream performance across diverse datasets and missing settings compared with existing baselines.

</details>


### [106] [Efficient Mean Curvature Computation on High-Dimensional Data Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06329)
*Alexandre L. M. Levada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对高维数据集中局部平均曲率计算昂贵的问题，提出两项互补贡献：一是通过代数恒等式将每点计算成本从O(m⁴)降至O(m²)；二是通过截断SVD和Haar测度下的解析近似，将总成本降至O(k²m + kmp²)，实验证实加速50-300倍且精度损失可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维数据集中计算每个点的局部平均曲率是几何感知机器学习算法（如MCBP方法）的关键，但朴素实现基于k近邻局部形状算子近似，需显式构造矩阵H并计算迹，导致每点O(m⁴)的计算成本，使得该方法在特征维度超过几十时变得不可行。

Method: 1) 利用协方差矩阵特征向量的正交性和迹算子的循环性，推导精确代数恒等式，消除矩阵H，将每点成本降至O(m²)；2) 针对O(m³)的特征分解瓶颈，利用局部协方差矩阵秩至多为k-1的事实，将其替换为k×m中心化数据矩阵的截断SVD（O(k²m)），并基于Haar测度下外积的期望值，推导零空间特征向量贡献的解析近似，最终总成本为O(k²m + kmp²)。

Result: 在真实数据集上实现50-300倍的加速，快速估计器替代原始版本时精度损失可忽略。

Conclusion: 通过提供可扩展且数据驱动的局部曲率估计，该方法将曲率确立为从经典到现代深度学习管道中广泛机器学习任务的实用几何特征。

Abstract: Estimating local mean curvature at each point of a high-dimensional dataset is a key ingredient of geometry-aware machine learning algorithms, such as the Mean Curvature Boundary Points (MCBP) method. The naive implementation of this computation, based on a local shape operator approximated from k-nearest neighbor patches, involves an explicit construction of a matrix $H$ whose trace form yields an $O(m^4)$ cost per point, rendering the approach intractable for datasets with more than a few dozen features. This paper introduces two complementary contributions that together reduce this cost by several orders of magnitude. The first contribution is an exact algebraic identity. This identity, derived from the orthogonality of the eigenvectors of the covariance matrix and the cyclicity of the trace operator, eliminates $H$ entirely and reduces the per-point cost to $O(m^2)$ after the eigendecomposition. The second contribution addresses the remaining $O(m^3)$ bottleneck of the full eigendecomposition. Since the local covariance matrix has rank at most $k-1 \ll m$, we replace it with a truncated SVD of the $k \times m$ centered data matrix, an $O(k^2 m)$ operation, and derive an analytical approximation for the contribution of the null-space eigenvectors based on the expected value of their outer product under the Haar measure. The resulting estimator has total cost $O(k^2 m + k m p^2)$, where $p = k-1$. Experiments on real-world datasets confirm speedups of 50 to 300 times relative to the original implementation, with negligible loss when the fast estimator is used to replace the original version. By providing a scalable and data-driven estimate of local curvature, the proposed method establishes curvature as a practical geometric feature for a broad range of machine learning tasks, from classical to modern deep learning pipelines.

</details>


### [107] [Subspace-Aware Sparse Autoencoders for Effective Mechanistic Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06333)
*Seyed Arshan Dalili,Mehrdad Mahdavi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出现有稀疏自编码器（SAE）将每个潜在特征假设为一维方向与LLM中特征的多维结构不匹配，导致特征分裂；提出子空间感知稀疏自编码器（SASA），使用学习到的子空间解码器、Top-s组门控和核范数正则化，在GPT-2和Mistral-7B上减少了特征分裂和吸收，提高了单义性和可解释性，且仅需约一半的训练token即可匹配或超越标准SAE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准SAE将每个潜在特征分配单一解码器方向，隐含特征是一维的假设，这与大语言模型中特征固有的多维结构不匹配，导致特征分裂（feature splitting）问题，阻碍了可解释性分析的准确性。

Method: 1）理论分析：从几何角度证明在内在维度d_i≥2时，单方向解码器需要指数级数量的原子才能达到误差ε；从优化角度证明存在连续路径使SAE目标风险严格降低。2）提出SASA：用学习到的子空间替代单向量解码器，通过Top-s组门控强制块稀疏，使用核范数正则化自适应调整组的有效秩。

Result: 在GPT-2和Mistral-7B上的实验表明，SASA有效减少了特征分裂和吸收现象，提升了单义性和可解释性，在仅使用约一半训练token的情况下，性能匹配或超越了标准SAE。

Conclusion: 特征的一维假设是多维模型特征分裂的根本原因；SASA通过子空间解码器提供了理论上更优的解决方案，将样本复杂度从指数级降低为多项式级，为大规模LLM的机械可解释性提供了更高效的工具。

Abstract: Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) are widely used for mechanistic interpretability in large language models, yet their formulation assigns each latent feature a single decoder direction, implicitly assuming features to be one-dimensional. We show that this assumption mismatches with the multi-dimensional structure of model features, provably inducing feature splitting through two distinct mechanisms. Geometrically, reconstructing a feature of intrinsic dimension $d_i \ge 2$ to error $\varepsilon$ with single-direction decoders forces a number of atoms that is exponential in $d_i$. From an end-to-end optimization perspective, this splitting is not merely possible but actively preferred. We prove that there exists a continuous path from the true $d_i$-dimensional basis to a strictly lower risk of the $\ell_1$-regularized SAE objective, whose descent directions drive any trained dictionary into that exponential regime. A single coherent feature is therefore fragmented across many near-collinear latents, producing spurious multiplicity and obscuring the intrinsic geometry. Motivated by this, we introduce Subspace-Aware Sparse Autoencoders (SASA), which replace single-vector decoders with learned decoder subspaces, enforce block sparsity via Top-$s$ group gating, and adapt each group's effective rank with a nuclear-norm regularizer. We then show that once the block size satisfies $r \ge d_i$, a single group not only can represent the entire feature slice but is the global minimizer of the SASA objective. This consolidation yields a sample complexity polynomial in $d_i$ rather than exponential -- a decisive advantage given that every training activation costs an LLM forward pass. Empirically, on GPT-2 and Mistral-7B, SASA reduces feature splitting and absorption, improves monosemanticity and interpretability, and matches or exceeds standard SAEs while training on roughly half the token budget.

</details>


### [108] [Quantifying the Privacy of Counterfactuals by Leveraging Membership Inference Attacks Against Synthetic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06334)
*Maryam Babaei,Yingke Wang,Hadrien Lautraite,Heber H. Arcolezi,Ulrich Aivodji,Sebastien Gambs*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文论证了反事实解释（counterfactuals）可以像合成数据一样被对手利用，通过针对合成数据的成员推理攻击方法来攻击反事实数据，从而泄露模型训练数据的隐私。研究发现，即使在没有模型访问权限的情况下，仅凭反事实数据本身也能成功实施成员推理攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反事实解释通常用于高风险决策领域，通过展示如何改变用户特征以得到期望结果来解释模型。然而，这种解释可能被对手利用进行隐私攻击。作者类比反事实数据与合成数据的相似性，探索是否能将针对合成数据的隐私攻击方法迁移到反事实数据上。

Method: 作者借鉴了针对合成数据设计的成员推理攻击方法，将其应用于各类反事实数据；同时提出了在无需访问原始模型的情况下，仅利用反事实数据本身进行成员推理攻击的方法。

Result: 实验表明，针对合成数据的成员推理攻击能够有效应用于反事实数据，并且即使没有模型查询权限，仅凭反事实数据本身也能成功实施成员推理攻击。

Conclusion: 模型开发者在发布反事实数据时应更加谨慎，因为反事实数据的发布可能导致隐私泄露风险。

Abstract: Counterfactuals are typically used in high-stakes decision areas to explain a machine learning model by showing how changes to the user profiles result in the desired outcome. However, explaining the model's decisions through counterfactuals can also be exploited by an adversary to conduct privacy attacks against the model or its training data. Drawing on the analogy that counterfactuals provide realistic substitutes for real training data, similar to synthetic data, we demonstrate in this paper how it is possible to successfully perform privacy attacks on counterfactuals by drawing on the attacks developed against synthetic data. More precisely, we investigate the effectiveness of the membership inference attacks designed for synthetic data on various types of counterfactuals. Additionally, while existing membership inference attacks against counterfactuals usually require to be able to query the model, we show how it is possible to perform successful membership inference attacks using only a set of counterfactuals, with no access to the model from which they are generated. Our results demonstrate that model developers should be more cautious when releasing counterfactuals to various users, as it can lead to a privacy breach.

</details>


### [109] [Bridging Domain Expertise and Generalization for Performance Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06335)
*Shuxuan Li,Zhilin Zhao,Quyu Kong,Wei-Shi Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FRAP方法，通过融合基础模型与外部基础模型的互补优势，构建更可靠的伪标签参考分布，从而在分布偏移下更准确地估计模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布偏移下的性能估计方法仅依赖目标模型输出，当分布发生偏移时模型偏差会被放大，导致与真实性能的相关性减弱。

Method: FRAP首先对基础模型进行温度缩放校准，使其预测分布与基础模型的分布散度最小化；然后通过置信度加权融合两个模型的预测，得到融合参考分布；最后计算基础模型预测与该参考分布的一致性作为性能估计。

Result: 在多种数据集和架构上的实验表明，FRAP在分布偏移下持续且显著优于现有的代表性性能估计方法。

Conclusion: 利用外部基础模型的鲁棒性与基础模型的领域专长进行互补融合，可以有效缓解分布偏移下模型偏差放大问题，提升性能估计的可靠性。

Abstract: Performance estimation under distribution shift aims to predict how a model behaves on an unlabeled test set whose distribution differs from the training data, a scenario that requires reliable indicators that can faithfully reflect model behavior without ground-truth labels. Existing approaches rely solely on the outputs of the given model whose biases are amplified once the distribution shifts, weakening the correlation with the true performance. Motivated by this limitation, we propose Fused Reference Alignment Prediction (FRAP), which leverages the complementary strengths of an external foundation model and the base model to construct a more reliable surrogate of the ground-truth labels. FRAP aligns the prediction distribution of the foundation model with that of the base model by applying temperature-scaled calibration that minimizes their divergence. The aligned predictions are fused through confidence-based weighting into a refined reference distribution that integrates robustness from the foundation model and domain-specific expertise from the base model, and performance estimation is obtained by measuring how closely the base model predictions agree with this reference. Extensive experiments across diverse datasets and architectures show that FRAP provides consistent and substantial improvements over representative performance-estimation methods under distribution shift.

</details>


### [110] [Equivariant Neural Belief Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06344)
*Zehua Cheng,Wei Dai,Jiahao Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出等变神经信念传播（ENBP），一种因子图框架，其消息为等变高斯混合模型，通过可微谱分解合成秩-2精度矩阵，并使用与SE(3)可交换的贪心KL混合约简保持可计算性。在GEOM-QM9和GEOM-Drugs上达到98.9%构象覆盖率、0.090Å误差和亚秒级延迟（比扩散基线快100倍以上）；在多体机器人推理中，当标准Loopy BP在15+智能体时发散，ENBP收敛且碰撞率近乎为零，等变误差达机器精度（~10⁻⁷ vs 10⁻¹）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有等变网络仅能输出标量和向量，无法生成各向异性不确定性所需的秩-2精度张量；单分量消息会将多模态能量景观坍塌为物理上无意义的平均值。需要一种能够处理SE(3)空间中多模态不确定性且保持严格等变性的推理框架。

Method: 提出Equivariant Neural Belief Propagation (ENBP)，其核心创新包括：(1) 消息为等变高斯混合模型，充分统计量在SE(3)下精确变换；(2) 通过等变外积合成秩-2精度矩阵；(3) 可微谱分解处理精度矩阵；(4) 贪心KL散度基的混合约简，且证明其与SE(3)可交换。

Result: GEOM-QM9和GEOM-Drugs：98.9%构象覆盖率，0.090Å误差，亚秒级延迟，比扩散基线快100倍以上且精度更高。多体机器人推理：标准Loopy BP在15+智能体时发散，ENBP收敛，碰撞率近乎为零，等变误差达机器精度（~10⁻⁷），对比增强基线为10⁻¹。

Conclusion: ENBP通过等变高斯混合消息和秩-2精度张量合成，成功实现了SE(3)等变的概率推理，在分子构象生成和多体机器人推理中显著优于现有方法，兼具高精度、高效率与严格等变性。

Abstract: Probabilistic inference over spatially embedded variables requires beliefs that respect $SE(3)$ symmetry, yet existing equivariant networks produce only scalars and vectors -- not the rank-2 precision tensors needed for anisotropic uncertainty, and single-component messages collapse multi-modal energy landscapes to physically meaningless averages. We introduce Equivariant Neural Belief Propagation (ENBP), a factor-graph framework whose messages are equivariant Gaussian mixture models with sufficient statistics that transform exactly under $SE(3)$. Rank-2 precision matrices are synthesised via equivariant outer products, ingested through differentiable spectral decomposition, and kept tractable by a greedy KL-based mixture reduction that provably commutes with $SE(3)$. On GEOM-QM9 and GEOM-Drugs, ENBP achieves 98.9% conformational coverage at 0.090 $\mathring{A}$ error with sub-second latency -- over $100\times$ faster than diffusion baselines at higher accuracy. On multi-body robotic inference, vanilla loopy BP diverges at 15+ agents while ENBP converges with near-zero collision rates and machine-precision equivariance error (${\sim}10^{-7}$ vs.\ $10^{-1}$ for augmented baselines).

</details>


### [111] [Performance Evaluation of GraphCast for Medium-Range Weather Forecasting over Brazil](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06348)
*Wolfgang R. Rowell,Lucas S. Kupssinskü*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究评估了GraphCast在巴西四个气候亚区域的预报性能，发现其技能呈现季节和区域依赖性：冬季对中尺度斜压系统预报能力不足，夏季对对流变率的平滑有助于改善温湿场预报，为AI天气模型在热带地区的应用建立了基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基于机器学习的天气预报模型（MLWP）在全球尺度表现优异，但在全球南方（如巴西）复杂、强对流环境中的有效性尚未得到充分验证，缺乏区域基准评估。

Method: 利用可扩展的云原生管道和WeatherBench-X框架，以IFS分析为真值，对比GraphCast与确定性ECMWF IFS HRES在巴西四个气候亚区域中850hPa温度、850hPa比湿和500hPa位势高度的预报统计指标。

Result: 冬季（2-7天）GraphCast在500hPa位势高度上对南巴西快速传播的斜压系统预报不如IFS，但在扩展预报期因平滑效应占优；夏季GraphCast能准确捕捉大尺度水汽输送，同时抑制高频对流变率，改善确定性NWP温度预报。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了巴西区域AI天气模型性能基线，明确了物理边界条件，为未来面向区域韧性的“热带化”（tropicalization）优化工作提供指导。

Abstract: The paradigm of global weather forecasting is rapidly shifting with the emergence of Machine Learning Weather Prediction models (MLWP). While these data-driven architectures demonstrate remarkable global skill, regional benchmarks in the Global South remain scarce, leaving their efficacy in complex, highly convective environments largely unverified. This study evaluates the performance of GraphCast operational against the deterministic ECMWF IFS HRES as baseline across four distinct Brazilian climatic sub-regions. Utilizing a scalable, cloud-native pipeline and the WeatherBench-X framework for benchmarking weather models, we assess selected tropospheric variables ($T_{850}$, $Q_{850}$, $Z_{500}$) over four selected seasonal windows, employing the operational IFS analysis as the ground truth to calculate the statistical metrics for both models. Results reveal a regime-dependent skill profile. During the austral winter, GraphCast underperforms in the medium range (lead days 2-7) for $Z_{500}$ when resolving fast-propagating baroclinic systems over southern Brazil, but regains an advantage in the extended range, where its inherent smoothing of chaotic small-scale variability becomes beneficial under deterministic skill metrics. Conversely, during the austral summer wet season, GraphCast accurately captures large-scale moisture transport while intrinsically dampening the high-frequency convective variability that degrades deterministic NWP temperature forecasts. These findings establish a baseline for Brazil and define the specific physical boundaries that will guide future ``tropicalization'' efforts, aiming to optimize these foundational AI models for regional resilience.

</details>


### [112] [Maximising the Set-Piece Return: Optimising Football Corner Tactics with Graph Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06353)
*Sean Groom,Michael Groom,Francisco Belo,Axl Rice,Liam Anderson,Victor-Alexandru Darvariu,Shuo Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于图强化学习的角球战术优化方法，该策略能调整任意起始球员位置和速度以最大化首次触球射门概率，在英超3000+角球数据上优于传统优化方法，将定位球分析从历史评估转向奖励驱动的战术发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有足球战术分析方法局限于表征历史动作或分析师指定的反事实场景，缺乏发现新的、可推广的球员配置和策略的能力。

Method: 将角球战术优化建模为决策问题，设计基于图结构数据的强化学习架构，使中央策略能够调整进攻球员的位置和速度，最大化首次触球射门概率。该方法适用于任意起始球员位置，而非仅针对孤立场景求解。

Result: 在超过3000个英超角球数据集上的评估显示，在匹配推理预算下，该方法显著优于基线优化技术。

Conclusion: 图强化学习能够将定位球分析从历史评估和模仿转向奖励驱动的战术发现。

Abstract: Machine learning is increasingly employed for the evaluation of football tactics. However, existing approaches focus on characterising historical actions or analyst-specified counterfactual scenarios. In this work, we seek to go beyond the imitation of historically observed patterns towards discovering new generalisable player configurations and strategies. To tackle this, we focus on optimising corner kick routines, and formulate a decision-making problem in which a central policy makes adjustments to attacking player positions and velocities to maximise first contact shot probability. Unlike classic optimisation that solves for isolated setups, we contribute a reinforcement learning architecture operating on graph-structured data that yields a general policy for adjusting arbitrary starting player positions. Evaluated on over 3,000 Premier League corners, our approach strongly outperforms baseline optimisation techniques under matched inference budgets. Our results suggest that graph reinforcement learning can shift set-piece analysis from historical evaluation and imitation towards reward-driven tactical discovery.

</details>


### [113] [End-to-End Subgraph Detection with GraphDETR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06364)
*Dexiong Chen,Till Hendrik Schulz,Karsten Borgwardt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GraphDETR是一种将子图检测建模为集合预测问题的深度学习框架，借鉴DETR思想，使用图神经网络编码目标图，结合Transformer解码器和可学习查询向量，通过端到端二分图匹配训练，一次性预测所有模式出现位置，支持精确匹配和近似匹配，在分子结构、环、团等多样模式检测中表现优异，在ChEMBL数据集上实现AP₁₀₀=91.2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 子图检测是科学领域的根本性问题，但传统基于子图同构的组合方法受限于NP完全问题，仅能处理小规模模式或中等规模图，缺乏可扩展性和灵活性。

Method: 提出GraphDETR框架，将子图检测视为集合预测问题。使用图神经网络编码目标图，采用固定数量的可学习查询向量，通过Transformer解码器联合预测所有模式出现，利用二分图匹配进行端到端训练。

Result: 能够检测多达50个节点的多样模式（分子结构、环、团、模糊模式），目标图规模可达1000个节点。在ChEMBL分子功能基团检测任务中，AP₁₀₀达到91.2。

Conclusion: GraphDETR不仅突破了传统组合方法在子图检测中的规模和复杂度限制，还天然支持近似匹配，为子图检测提供了可扩展的深度学习解决方案。

Abstract: Subgraph detection seeks to identify whether and where instances of query patterns occur within a larger graph. This problem is fundamental across scientific domains and is closely related to subgraph isomorphism, which is NP-complete, limiting combinatorial approaches to small patterns or moderately sized graphs. We introduce GraphDETR, a deep learning framework that formulates subgraph detection as a set prediction problem, analogous to DETR in object detection. GraphDETR encodes the target graph with a graph neural network, and employs a fixed set of learnable query vectors, decoded via a transformer decoder, to predict all pattern occurrences jointly in a single forward pass. This is enabled by training the model end-to-end with bipartite matching. Unlike traditional combinatorial methods that only solve exact structural matching, GraphDETR naturally extends to approximate matching, enabling detection beyond exact pattern correspondence. Empirically, we show that GraphDETR can detect diverse patterns, such as molecular structures, cycles, cliques, and fuzzy patterns of up to 50 nodes, in target graphs with up to 1000 nodes. We further evaluate on molecular functional group detection over the ChEMBL dataset, where GraphDETR predicts the complete set of functional groups per molecule, achieving a strong performance of $\text{AP}_{100} = 91.2$.

</details>


### [114] [Learned Response-Field Inertia Operator for HEC-RAS 2D Water-Surface Elevation Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06385)
*Edward Holmberg,Elias Ioup,Md Meftahul Ferdaus,Mahdi Abdelguerfi,Julian Simeonov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LRFIO，一种无需外部强迫、基于增量学习的原生网格替代模型，用于HEC-RAS 2D水面高程预测。该模型通过保留惯性响应算子进行闭式滚动预测，在不同基准测试中自适应选择复杂度，最大验证遗憾4.30%，部署时间0.003–0.242秒，比HEC-RAS快约2.75×10^4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有替代模型通常在重采样网格上评估，引入栅格重映射误差和信息访问混淆问题。本文旨在直接对原始非均匀计算单元进行评估，并引入一种可自适应选择复杂度的轻量级替代模型，在保证精度的前提下大幅提升计算效率。

Method: 提出LRFIO框架：基于无强迫、增量学习的替代模型，从已有HEC-RAS轨迹中校准惯性响应算子，然后通过闭式原生网格滚动部署。采用"基情况优先"响应层次（持久性→全局校准惯性→分段响应场惯性），将分割、残差校正和神经化惯性视为可学习建模选择，仅当验证证据支持时才保留额外复杂度。

Result: 在四个不同HEC-RAS 2D基准测试上，LRFIO针对不同域保留不同响应结构，实现自适应复杂度控制；最大验证遗憾4.30%；部署滚动时间0.003–0.242秒；Beaver Bayou实测对比中，归一化加速比约2.75×10^4倍。

Conclusion: 当前原生网格增量是强有力的求解器条件预测支架；添加响应场、神经或空间复杂度应仅在经验证据支撑时才保留。

Abstract: This article presents a cross-dataset evaluation of learned native-cell surrogate models for solver-consistent water-surface elevation (WSE) prediction in HEC-RAS 2D. To avoid raster remapping error and information-access confounding, surrogates are evaluated directly on the original nonuniform computational cells under an explicit policy that separates static project inputs, current hydraulic state, project-input forcing, calibration-derived quantities, and future solver-output targets. We introduce the Learned Response-Field Inertia Operator (LRFIO), a no-forcing, increment-based learned surrogate that calibrates an inertial response operator from solved HEC-RAS trajectories and deploys the retained operator through closed-form native-cell rollout. LRFIO evaluates a base-case-first response hierarchy consisting of persistence, global calibrated inertia, and segmented response-field inertia. Segmentation, residual correction, and neuralized inertia are treated as learnable modeling choices, with added complexity retained only when validation evidence justifies its cost. Evaluated across four diverse HEC-RAS 2D benchmarks, LRFIO retains different response structures for different domains, demonstrating adaptive learned complexity. The selector audit shows controlled complexity with a maximum validation regret of 4.30%. During deployment, retained rollout times range from 0.003 s to 0.242 s, and the Beaver Bayou measured-solve comparison gives an estimated 2.75 x 10^4 horizon-normalized speedup over HEC-RAS. These results indicate that the current native-cell increment is a strong solver-conditioned predictive scaffold and that added response-field, neural, or spatial complexity should be retained only when empirically justified.

</details>


### [115] [Proper Scoring Rules for Right-Censored Survival Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06393)
*Jef Jonkers,Glenn Van Wallendael,Luc Duchateau,Sofie Van Hoecke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对右删失生存数据的通用评分框架，通过将预测分布映射到删失机制下，构建了局部化和边际化的适当评分规则，涵盖似然、IPCW、CRPS、Pinball损失、Brier分数和能量分数等，并验证了其适当地性与严格适当地性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的适当评分规则无法直接应用于右删失生存数据，因为事件时间仅被部分观测。需要一套在删失场景下仍保持统计适当地性的评分框架，以支持概率预测的训练与评估。

Method: 先通过删失机制将预测分布映射为观测数据分布，再对观测数据分布应用原有的适当评分规则。针对固定删失时间构建局部化分数，针对随机删失时间构建边际化分数。该原理进一步衍生出用于多变量右删失生存建模的样本级学习目标——删失回归（censored engression）。

Result: 在多种删失机制下，所提评分能够正确排序oracle预测，而依赖预测的插件式加权评分可能出现排序反转。删失回归在删失数据上的表现显著优于朴素训练方法。

Conclusion: 该框架统一了右删失场景下的多种评分准则，提供了理论保障（条件独立删失下适当地，可识别区域上严格适当地），并有效支持了删失生存建模的训练与评估。

Abstract: Proper scoring rules provide a rigorous theoretical basis for the training and evaluation of probabilistic forecasts. However, in the presence of right censoring, the event time is only partially observed, rendering conventional scoring rules inapplicable in their standard form. We propose a framework for proper scoring of right-censored survival outcomes based on a simple idea: first, map the predictive distribution through the censoring mechanism, then apply the underlying proper score on the induced observed-data law. This yields localized scores for fixed censoring times and marginalized scores when the censoring time is random or only partially observed. The resulting construction recovers familiar right-censored likelihood and IPCW-type criteria within a coherent framework, while also yielding right-censored versions of the CRPS, pinball loss, Brier score, and energy score. We show that the marginalized score is proper under conditional independent censoring and strictly proper on the identifiable region. The same principle also leads to censored engression, a sample-based learning objective for multivariate right-censored survival modeling. In experiments, our scores correctly rank the oracle forecast across several censoring regimes, whereas forecast-dependent plug-in weighted scores can exhibit ranking reversals. Censored engression likewise substantially improves over naive training on censored outcomes.

</details>


### [116] [The Post-GCN Decade Revisited: Curvature-Stratified Evaluation of Relational Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06397)
*Shuo Wang,Xiangyu Wang,Quanxin Wang,Bailin Wu,Bokui Wang,Shunyang Huang,Boyan Deng,Haonan Liu,Ruiyi Fang,Zhenxiang Xu,Boyu Wang,Zhao Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出当前关系学习中的评估方法（平均性能榜单）忽略了数据集的几何结构差异，存在系统性偏差。作者提出曲率分层评估框架，将数据集按正曲率、负曲率和近零曲率划分，评估了18个模型（GCN、GFM、表格学习等）在14个数据集上的表现，发现模型排名在曲率域内高度稳定但在域间显著变化，性能本质上是几何依赖的而非通用可迁移的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前关系学习的评估实践中广泛使用扁平化榜单对异质数据集进行平均性能排名，这种做法隐含地假设了数据具有统一的底层结构。作者证明该假设引入系统性偏差：掩盖了几何依赖的性能变化，并可能对模型泛化能力产生误导性结论。

Method: 提出曲率分层的评估框架，将数据集按内在几何曲率分为正曲率、负曲率和近零曲率三个机制域；选取18个代表性模型（包括GCN、图基础模型GFM和表格学习方法）在14个数据集上进行系统评估。

Result: 模型排名在同一曲率域内高度稳定，但在不同曲率域间出现显著变化；识别出GFM在某些曲率域中相比几何对齐的GNN出现收益递减的情况。

Conclusion: 性能本质上是几何依赖的而非通用可迁移的，因此提出几何感知的评估协议以替代标准聚合榜单，从而获得更可靠和可解释的比较结果。

Abstract: Current evaluation practices in relational learning rely heavily on flat leaderboards that average performance across heterogeneous datasets, implicitly assuming a uniform underlying structure. We show that this assumption introduces systematic bias: it obscures geometry-dependent performance variations and can lead to misleading conclusions about model generalization. In this work, we identify intrinsic geometry as a key latent factor governing model effectiveness. We demonstrate that conventional aggregated metrics mask critical performance trade-offs that only become visible when datasets are stratified by their geometric properties. To address this issue, we introduce a curvature-stratified evaluation framework that partitions datasets into positive, negative, and near-zero curvature regimes. Our benchmark evaluates 18 representative models including Graph Convolutional Networks (GCNs), Graph Foundation Models (GFMs), and tabular learning methods across 14 datasets. We find that model rankings are highly stable within each curvature regime but shift significantly across regimes, indicating that performance is fundamentally geometry-dependent rather than universally transferable. Notably, we identify regimes where GFMs offer diminishing returns compared to geometry-aligned GNNs. Based on these findings, we propose a geometry-aware evaluation protocol that yields more reliable and interpretable comparisons than standard aggregated benchmarks. We release all code, curvature-stratified dataset splits, and evaluation tools to support reproducible and rigorous assessment of future relational learning methods. Code and datasets are provided in our project homepage: https://sirbabbage.github.io/CurvBench_HOME/.

</details>


### [117] [Double Preconditioning (DoPr): Optimization for Test-Time Performance, not Validation Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06418)
*Thomas T. Zhang,Alok Shah,Yifei Zhang,Vincent Zhang,Nikolai Matni,Max Simchowitz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出双重预处理（DoPr）优化范式，通过结合梯度级预处理（如Adam/Muon）与激活级预处理（如KFAC），专门应对深度学习中测试时反馈（TTF）导致的误差累积问题，在自回归语言建模、流生成模型和机器人策略学习等场景中提升下游性能，且验证损失改善并不总能伴随测试性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习中许多模型通过一步预测损失（如L2回归、交叉熵）训练，但在部署时需沿自身预测展开（如自回归语言建模、流生成、机器人策略学习），导致训练/验证损失与下游指标（任务成功率、生成质量）之间存在显著不匹配（即测试时反馈TTF现象），且任务越长差距越大。现有方法多从数据、架构、目标函数角度缓解，本文首次从优化设计角度切入。

Method: 提出双重预处理（DoPr）优化范式，将梯度级预处理（如Adam、Muon）与激活级预处理（如KFAC）相结合。激活级预处理作为即插即用的干预手段，在训练过程中同步调节参数更新方向。

Result: DoPr在多种TTF场景（自回归语言建模、流生成、机器人策略学习）中一致提升下游测试性能，且验证损失的改善并不总能伴随测试性能提升，揭示了现有评估方式的局限性。

Conclusion: 优化是缓解TTF问题的一个有效新设计维度；DoPr作为一种通用优化范式，在测试时反馈场景下优于仅使用梯度级预处理的优化器；验证损失与下游性能不完全一致，需重新审视TTF场景下的模型评估标准。

Abstract: Many modern applications of deep learning involve training a neural network via a one-step prediction loss (e.g., $L^2$ regression, cross-entropy), but deploy the network by rolling out along its own predictions. Key examples include autoregressive language modeling, flow-based generative modeling, and robot policy learning. It is well-documented that these settings induce a phenomenon we call test-time feedback (TTF): the mismatch between the training/validation loss and downstream metrics of interest, such as task success rate and generation quality, which grows with task length. While data curation, architecture, and objective design have been proposed to combat train-test shift in TTF settings, this paper proposes optimization as a new design axis to mitigate error accumulation. Specifically, we introduce a new optimization paradigm called double-preconditioning (DoPr) uniquely tailored to the challenges of TTF. DoPr combines gradient-wise preconditioning, as in Adam and Muon, with activation-wise preconditioning (AP), such as in KFAC. We show that the addition of AP yields a drop-in intervention for increasing downstream model performance across a range of TTF settings. Interestingly, these gains in test-time performance do not consistently accompany improvements in validation loss, opening new questions about how to properly evaluate models trained with one-step supervised objectives.

</details>


### [118] [Causal Atlases from Entropic Inference: Bayesian Networks beyond Optimal DAGs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06440)
*Hazhir Aliahmadi,Irina Babayan,Greg van Anders*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于熵的推理方法生成因果图谱集，以量化贝叶斯网络中因数据变异性导致的因果结构模糊性，并证明传统优化方法得到的DAG可能包含伪影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统贝叶斯网络构建依赖优化方法，但底层数据可能存在多条因果链，优化方法无法忠实表征数据内在的变异性，导致因果伪影。

Method: 对2节点和20节点的线性结构方程模型模拟含噪数据，采样最大熵图集合并量化因果结构的内在模糊性。

Result: 优化得到的DAG包含不一致的因果伪影；基于熵的推理能生成与数据一致的多重因果图谱。

Conclusion: 基于熵的推理方法优于传统优化方法，能更忠实地表示数据中固有的因果不确定性。

Abstract: Data-driven causal relationship identification is pertinent to advancing understanding of complex systems both within and beyond science. Bayesian networks offer a probabilistic method for modelling generic causal relationships via directed acyclic graphs (DAGs). However, typical techniques for constructing Bayesian networks rely on optimization, which can be ill-suited for learning causal relationships because the underlying data may admit multiple chains of causation. More data-faithful representations of causal relationships would provide frameworks for constructing multiple causal maps that are consistent with the variability that is inherent in underlying data. Here, we show that entropy-based inference generates atlases of plausible causal relationships that are consistent with underlying data. On simulated noisy data of 2- and 20-node linear structural equation models, we sample a maximum-entropy ensemble of graphs that allow us to quantify the inherent structural ambiguity in underlying causal relationships. Our method shows that "optimized" DAGs can contain causal artifacts are not consistent across equivalently accurate topologies.

</details>


### [119] [In-Context Multiple Instance Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06458)
*Alexander Möllers,Marvin Sextro,Julius Hense,Gabriel Dernbach,Klaus-Robert Müller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Perceiver架构的上下文学习器，通过在合成数据上预训练，使其能够从少量标注包中解决新的多实例学习任务，无需梯度更新，并在12个MIL基准测试中取得最佳平均性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MIL算法在低标签数据场景下表现不佳，灵活模型容易过拟合而刚性模型难以适应任务需求，亟需一种能从小样本中快速泛化的方法。

Method: 采用Perceiver架构作为上下文学习器，在合成数据上进行预训练；设计并研究了多种针对包结构数据的合成数据生成器，发现它们捕获互补的归纳偏差；推理时通过单次前向传播完成分类，无需梯度更新。

Result: 在12个MIL基准测试中，预训练模型平均性能最佳，超越了需要任务特定训练的有监督基线方法。

Conclusion: 混合多种合成数据生成器进行预训练，可使模型继承各生成器的优势，从而在小样本MIL任务中实现高效泛化。

Abstract: Multiple Instance Learning (MIL) addresses problems where supervision is available at the level of bags of instances and has been successfully applied in fields ranging from computational pathology to satellite imagery. Nevertheless, existing algorithms struggle in the low-label regime that characterizes many real-world applications. Flexible models overfit and rigid ones fail to adapt to the task at hand. We show that pretraining an in-context learner with a Perceiver-style architecture on synthetic data yields a model that can solve new tasks from a handful of labeled bags. At inference time, classification happens in a single forward pass and requires no gradient updates. We propose and investigate different synthetic data generators for bag-structured data and find that they capture complementary inductive biases. A model pretrained on a mixture of these generators inherits their per-task strengths and achieves the best average performance across twelve MIL benchmarks, outperforming supervised baselines that require task-specific training.

</details>


### [120] [Event Detection for Parameter-to-KPI Dependency Learning for AI-RAN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06459)
*Christie Djidjev,Nicholas Kaminski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对AI-RAN/O-RAN架构中多个AI驱动控制功能相互干扰的问题，提出了一种将连续遥测数据转换为二元事件指示器的显著性检测方法，并构建了合成闭环流量生成器来评估依赖恢复管线，实验表明该方法在信噪比足够时能可靠恢复潜在依赖结构，阈值校准是关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 下一代无线网络中多个AI驱动的控制功能同时优化不同网络目标时会产生相互干扰，而现有方法难以从原始网络数据中检测这种交互关系，缺乏可靠的、可解释的依赖结构来捕捉控制参数与网络性能结果之间的主动影响关系。

Method: 将连续遥测数据转换为二元事件指示器的过程建模为显著性检测问题；引入了一个合成的闭环流量生成器（含植入的潜在依赖关系）来规避真实AI-RAN流量中难以获取参数-KPI真实标签的问题；使用该受控遥测数据评估基于机器学习的依赖恢复管线。

Result: 当信号与背景变化有足够分离度时，所提管线能从含噪连续轨迹中可靠恢复潜在依赖结构；阈值校准是控制事件检测质量的关键因素。

Conclusion: 这项工作为自适应AI-RAN控制系统的可解释依赖学习奠定了基础，是实现可解释依赖学习的关键第一步。

Abstract: Next-generation wireless networks are expected to rely on multiple concurrent AI-driven control functions that optimize different network objectives simultaneously, particularly in AI-integrated and open radio access network architectures such as AI Radio Access Network (AI-RAN) and Open Radio Access Network (O-RAN). When these functions interact, they can interfere with one another in ways that are difficult to detect from raw network data alone. A key missing piece for managing such interactions is a reliable, interpretable dependency structure that captures which control parameters are actively influencing which network performance outcomes at any given time. This paper focuses on the event-detection step needed to support such dependency learning by converting noisy continuous telemetry into binary indicators of parameter activity and KPI response. The central difficulty is that not every fluctuation in the data reflects a genuine control interaction, so the method must distinguish real parameter-outcome relationships from background variation. Because real AI-RAN traffic traces with known parameter-KPI ground truth are difficult to obtain, we introduce a synthetic closed-loop traffic generator with planted latent dependencies. We use this controlled telemetry to evaluate a machine-learning-based dependency recovery pipeline that formulates the conversion of continuous traces into binary event indicators as a significance-detection problem. Experimental evaluation shows that the proposed pipeline reliably recovers the latent dependency structure from noisy continuous traces when the signal is sufficiently separated from background variation, while highlighting threshold calibration as the key factor controlling event-detection quality. These results constitute a foundational step toward interpretable dependency learning for adaptive AI-RAN control systems.

</details>


### [121] [PC Layer: Polynomial Weight Preconditioning for Improving LLM Pre-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06470)
*Senmiao Wang,Tiantian Fang,Haoran Zhang,Yushun Zhang,Kunxiang Zhao,Alex Schwing,Ruoyu Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出预条件（PC）层，通过多项式预条件器对权重矩阵进行参数化，在LLM训练中保持权重条件稳定，可合并回原架构且无推理开销。实验在Llama-1B预训练中验证了优于标准Transformer，理论上证明了均匀约束各层奇异值可确保深度线性网络的梯度下降几何收敛到全局最小值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决大语言模型（LLM）训练中权重矩阵条件数不稳定导致的优化困难问题。

Method: 提出PC层，利用低阶多项式预条件器重塑权重矩阵的奇异值谱，训练后可将预条件权重合并回原始架构，不引入推理开销。

Result: 在Llama-1B预训练中，PC层在使用AdamW和Muon优化器时均优于标准Transformer。

Conclusion: 均匀约束各层奇异值可确保深度线性网络梯度下降几何收敛到全局最小值，PC层在LLM训练中有效且高效。

Abstract: We propose a preconditioning (PC) layer, a weight parameterization via polynomial preconditioner that ensures stable weight conditioning throughout LLM training. The PC module reshapes the singular-value spectrum of weight matrices via low-degree polynomial preconditioning. After training, the preconditioned weights can be merged back into the original architecture, incurring no inference overhead. We demonstrate the advantage of the proposed PC layer over standard transformers in Llama-1B pre-training, for both the AdamW and Muon optimizers. Theoretically, we justify this spectrum-control principle by proving that uniformly bounding each layer's singular values ensures geometric convergence of gradient descent to global minima, for certain deep linear networks. Our code is available at https://github.com/Empath-aln/PC-layer.

</details>


### [122] [RREDCoT: Segment-Level Reward Redistribution for Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06475)
*Mykyta Ielanskyi,Kajetan Schweighofer,Lukas Aichberger,Sepp Hochreiter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对GRPO等强化学习微调方法在推理语言模型中面临的延迟奖励和高方差问题，本文提出RREDCoT，利用模型自身进行奖励重新分配，无需额外生成即可近似最优信用分配，从而提升CoT训练效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL微调方法（如GRPO）依赖蒙特卡洛采样估计中间状态值，存在高方差且计算开销大，不适合长序列CoT的细粒度信用分配。需要一种高效的奖励重新分配方法来解决延迟奖励问题。

Method: 提出RREDCoT方法，利用模型自身（无需额外生成）来近似最优奖励重新分配，将CoT轨迹分段后，对重要片段分配更高奖励，实现细粒度的信用分配。

Result: 与蒙特卡洛采样和多种归因方法相比，RREDCoT在奖励重新分配效率上更具优势，同时分析了CoT分段粒度和状态值估计等因素对效果的影响。

Conclusion: RREDCoT通过模型自带的近似能力实现高效奖励重新分配，为解决推理语言模型中延迟奖励问题提供了可行的替代方案，降低了训练方差和计算开销。

Abstract: Recent advancements in reasoning language models have been driven by Reinforcement Learning (RL) fine-tuning. Most often, these rely on the Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) algorithm or modifications thereof to steer the models to produce Chain-of-Thought (CoT) traces. The final answer can only be verified, and the reward assigned, after the CoT trace is complete, making it a delayed reward problem. GRPO and its modifications correspond to Monte Carlo methods in standard RL, which are known to suffer from high variance. A possible solution to this problem is the redistribution of rewards through credit assignment, where segments of the CoT trace that are important for arriving at the desirable solution are emphasized by assigning a higher reward. While Monte Carlo sampling can be used to provide an unbiased estimate of intermediate state values, its computational overhead makes it unsuitable for train-time credit assignment in long contexts at high granularity. We introduce RREDCoT (Reward REDistribution for Chain of Thoughts), which utilizes the model itself to approximate the optimal reward redistribution without additional generation. We investigate the advantages of our method compared to MC sampling and several attribution methods. We further analyze several aspects relevant to the construction of the redistribution such as segmentation of CoT traces and state value estimation.

</details>


### [123] [Pretraining Recurrent Networks without Recurrence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06479)
*Akarsh Kumar,Phillip Isola*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出监督记忆训练(SMT)，通过将RNN训练转化为一步记忆过渡标签上的监督学习，避免了循环信用分配，实现了时间并行且梯度路径恒定的RNN训练，在语言建模等任务上优于BPTT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准BPTT存在时间顺序限制（并行性差）和梯度消失/爆炸问题，难以学习长程依赖关系。

Method: SMT：1) 用基于Transformer的编码器在预测状态目标上训练，生成记忆标签；2) 将RNN训练转化为一步记忆过渡标签$(m_t, x_{t+1}) \rightarrow m_{t+1}$上的监督学习；3) 解耦"记住什么"与"如何更新记忆"，实现时间并行训练和恒定$O(1)$梯度路径。

Result: 在多种RNN架构上的语言建模和像素序列建模任务中，SMT优于BPTT；能更好地捕捉长程依赖并实现并行训练。

Conclusion: SMT有望解锁构建时间抽象模型的可扩展训练，为RNN的高效并行训练提供新范式。

Abstract: Training recurrent neural networks (RNNs) requires assigning credit across long sequences of computations. Standard backpropagation through time (BPTT) addresses this problem poorly: it is sequential in time, limiting parallelism, and suffers from vanishing or exploding gradients, making long-range associations difficult to learn. We propose Supervised Memory Training (SMT), a method for training nonlinear RNNs that sidesteps recurrent credit propagation entirely by reducing RNN training to supervised learning on one-step memory transition labels $(m_t, x_{t+1}) \rightarrow m_{t+1}$. SMT acquires these memory labels by training a Transformer-based encoder on a predictive state objective--retaining only information from the past necessary to predict the future. By decoupling what to remember from how to update memory, SMT enables time-parallel RNN training with a stable $O(1)$ length gradient path between any two tokens--without ever unrolling the RNN. We find that SMT outperforms BPTT when pretraining various RNN architectures on tasks like language modeling and pixel sequence modeling. SMT enables nonlinear RNNs to better capture long-range dependencies and train in parallel, potentially unlocking the scaling of models that build temporal abstractions of past experience.

</details>


### [124] [Regret Minimization with Adaptive Opponents in Repeated Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06486)
*Mingyang Liu,Asuman Ozdaglar,Tiancheng Yu,Kaiqing Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种适用于自适应对手的重复博弈新 regret 指标——Repeated Policy Regret (RP-Regret)，研究了其最小化的必要条件、算法设计与均衡学习能力，实验表明该指标能促成更合作的博弈结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统外部遗憾（external regret）无法有效衡量自适应对手场景下玩家的反事实推理能力，需要一种更适合重复博弈的 regret 指标来刻画玩家基于历史响应的策略比较。

Method: 提出 RP-Regret 定义，识别其亚线性增长的三个必要条件（比较器策略变化受限、比较器与对手的记忆限制），并设计了三种算法：基于优化 oracle 的非凸优化、凸线性化代理最小化、直接最小化（对手策略缓慢变化时）。

Result: 证明了 RP-Regret 亚线性最小化的必要条件；提出三种算法分别在不同假设下实现亚线性 regret；当所有玩家均最小化该 regret（或其线性化变体）时，可学习到重复博弈的特定子博弈完美均衡；实验显示在 Stag-Hunt 游戏中促进合作与更高效用。

Conclusion: RP-Regret 作为自适应对手场景下的合适指标，兼具理论可行性（亚线性 regret 可达、均衡可学）和实践有效性（促成合作博弈结果），为重复博弈中的在线学习提供了新框架。

Abstract: In this paper, we study regret minimization in repeated games with \emph{adaptive} opponents who can respond based on histories of play. The standard metric of \emph{external regret} in online learning is known to fail to capture such adaptivity. To account for players' counterfactual reasoning, we introduce {\tt Repeated Policy Regret (RP-Regret)}, a game-theoretic metric that measures the difference between the \emph{realized} and the \emph{best-in-hindsight} accumulated utility when all players can \emph{respond} to the history of play. Compared to existing regret notions in this setting, ours is native to repeated game playing, enabling stronger comparators and opponents with fewer constraints, while maintaining the possibility of finding better equilibria when all players minimize it. We first identify necessary conditions for obtaining {\tt RP-Regret} sublinear in time, on the variation of the player's comparator strategies in the regret definition and on the memories of both the comparator and opponents' strategies. We then study additional conditions and provable algorithms to minimize {\tt RP-Regret}, which is by definition \emph{non-convex} in the strategy space. To address this challenge, we propose three algorithms: (i) one based on an optimization oracle, as assumed in some prior work in online non-convex learning; (ii) one that minimizes a convex and \emph{linearized} surrogate of {\tt RP-Regret} at each iteration; (iii) one that directly minimizes {\tt RP-Regret} when opponents change strategies slowly. Furthermore, when all players can run algorithms to minimize the {\tt RP-Regret} (or its linearized variant), certain subgame perfect equilibria of the repeated game can be learned. We also provide experiments showing that minimizing our regret notions can lead to more cooperative solutions with higher utility in games such as Stag-Hunt.

</details>


### [125] [TailLoR: Protecting Principal Components in Parameter-Efficient Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06494)
*Marius Dragoi,Ioana Pintilie,Alexandra Dragomir,Antonio Barbalau,Florin Brad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TailLoR是一种基于谱分解的参数高效持续学习方法，利用预训练权重的奇异基作为固定参考框架，对奇异值矩阵进行低秩更新，并通过软谱惩罚将细粒度适应引导到长尾谱坐标中，以减少干扰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于谱分解的参数高效微调方法在持续学习中取得了进展，但现有方法在处理灾难性遗忘和干扰问题上仍存在不足，需要一种更有效的机制来平衡旧知识保留与新任务适应。

Method: 利用预训练权重的奇异基U和V作为固定参考框架，学习对奇异值矩阵的低秩更新；引入软谱惩罚机制，抑制与主导奇异方向对齐的更新，将细粒度适应路由到高度灵活的长尾谱坐标中。

Result: 通过软谱惩罚减少了对主导奇异方向的干扰，同时将适应性更新有效地分配到长尾谱坐标中，实现了更好的持续学习性能。

Conclusion: TailLoR通过将更新限制在奇异值矩阵的低秩空间中，并利用软谱惩罚引导适应向长尾谱坐标偏移，有效减少了任务间干扰，为持续学习中的参数高效微调提供了新范式。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient finetuning methods based on spectral decomposition have enabled progress in Continual Learning. In this paper we introduce TailLoR, which utilizes the singular bases U and V of the pre-trained weights as a fixed reference frame to learn a low-rank update applied to the singular value matrix. A soft spectral penalty discourages updates aligned with dominant singular directions, reducing interference while routing fine-grained adaptation into the highly flexible, long-tail spectral coordinates.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [126] [SET: Stream-Event-Triggered Scheduling for Efficient CUDA Graph Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05495)
*Zhengxiong Li,Tsung-Wei Huang,Umit Ogras*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出一种基于CUDA的多流任务并行流水线运行时框架，通过事件链式调度和工作窃取机制减少同步开销与调度延迟，结合图执行流和每流缓冲区保障内存安全，在真实负载上实现1.15-1.44倍加速，调度开销降低18-54%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU峰值性能受限于主机-设备同步延迟和内核调度开销，现有方法即使采用激进的内核优化和批处理仍难以消除这些瓶颈，且硬件资源（计算核心和拷贝引擎）因调度开销而未被充分利用。

Method: (1) 多流任务并行流水线编程模型，结合事件链式调度（event-chaining）和工作窃取（work-stealing）机制；(2) 基于图的执行流配合每流缓冲区（per-stream buffers），确保多个并发运行任务的内存安全。

Result: 在代表性真实工作负载上相比SOTA CUDA graph基线获得1.15-1.44倍加速，调度开销降低18-54%。

Conclusion: 提出的CUDA运行时框架有效最小化了同步开销和内核执行间隙，通过任务并行流水线设计充分释放GPU硬件资源潜力。

Abstract: Achieving peak GPU performance remains a significant challenge as the system throughput is constrained by host-device synchronization delays and kernel scheduling overheads, even with aggressive kernel optimizations and batch processing. Furthermore, existing approaches often underutilize hardware resources such as compute cores and copy engines due to scheduling overheads. To address these problems, we propose a CUDA runtime framework for task-parallel pipelines to minimize the synchronization overheads and the gap between kernel executions. The proposed solution combines two innovations: (1) a multi-stream task-parallel pipeline programming model that leverages event-chaining and work-stealing mechanisms to fully utilize available hardware resources; (2) a graph-based execution flow with per-stream buffers to ensure memory safety for multiple in-flight jobs running concurrently. Extensive evaluations on representative real-world workloads show 1.15--1.44X speedup and reduce scheduling overheads by 18--54% compared to state-of-the-art CUDA graph baselines.

</details>


### [127] [Beyond Greedy Chunking: SLO-Aware Sliding-Window Scheduling for LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05933)
*Yuansheng Chen,Yue Zhang,Xuan Mo,Weigang Wu,Jialun Li*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: SlidingServe是一个面向在线LLM推理的滑动窗口驱动、SLO感知调度系统，通过轻量级批延迟预测器、滑动分块器、多级优先级排序器和动态规划批量构造器，在保证严格QoS的前提下将服务容量提升高达30%，并将SLO违规率降低16%-53%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM服务系统依赖粗粒度的输出约束，难以有效处理多请求间的资源争用，导致资源利用率低且对细粒度QoS差异化的支持有限。

Method: 设计轻量级批延迟预测器估计批执行时间；提出SlidingChunker结合当前与下一次迭代信息实现动态分块；引入Multi-Level Priority Sorter排序候选请求以平衡公平性与效率；设计BatchConstructor使用动态规划选择当前轮次执行的请求集，降低关键请求的SLO违例风险。

Result: 在各种负载条件下，相比先进调度系统，服务容量提升高达30%；在重负载推理模式下，SLO违规率降低16%-53%。

Conclusion: SlidingServe通过滑动窗口驱动的细粒度SLO感知调度，有效提升了LLM在线推理系统的吞吐量与服务质量保障能力。

Abstract: With the rapid growth of interactive applications in large language model (LLM) online services, maintaining high system throughput while ensuring user-perceived latency has become a key issue in inference scheduling. Existing LLM service systems rely on coarse-grained output constraints, making it difficult to effectively handle resource contention among multiple requests, resulting in low resource utilization efficiency and limited support for fine-grained quality of service (QoS) differentiation. We present SlidingServe, a sliding-window-driven SLO-Aware scheduling system for online LLM inference. SlidingServe designed a lightweight batch latency predictor to estimate the execution time of a batch. Based on this, SlidingServe uses SlidingChunker to combine information from the current iteration and the next iteration to achieve dynamic chunking and improve the overall system throughput while maintaining strict QoS guarantees. SlidingServe introduces Multi-Level Priority Sorter to sort candidate requests in order to balance fairness and efficiency. Additionally, when multiple requests within the same batch are at risk of SLO violating,SlidingServe introduces BatchConstructor, which uses dynamic programming to select the set of requests to execute in the current round, mitigating the SLO violation risk of critical requests.Our evaluation demonstrates that SlidingServe can improve service capacity by up to 30% compared to advanced scheduling systems under various load conditions, and further reduces the rate of SLO violation by 16%-53% under heavy-load inference mode.

</details>


### [128] [Demystifying NVSHMEM: A System-Level Analysis on Symmetric Memory and Device-Initiated Operations in GPU Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05951)
*Yijun Ma,Siyuan Shen,Tiancheng Chen,Akhil Langer,Jiri Kraus,Benjamin Glick,Craig Belusar,Jeff Hammond,Torsten Hoefler*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了NVSHMEM——NVIDIA基于OpenSHMEM的PGAS通信库，重点分析了其对称内存模型、单边通信和设备端集合操作，并通过DeepEP案例展示了其在稀疏深度学习工作负载中的应用，表明NVSHMEM开创的设备端对称内存编程模型能够实现细粒度GPU驱动通信，对接近硬件性能极限至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管NVSHMEM在GPU集群中被越来越多地采用，但其设计和行为的系统级理解分散在文档、源代码和应用经验中，缺乏系统性整合与分析。

Method: 通过系统梳理NVSHMEM的编程模型、实现机制和性能特征，重点分析对称内存、单边操作和设备端集合操作，并以DeepEP作为实际案例进行深入剖析。

Result: NVSHMEM开创了设备端对称内存编程模型，支持细粒度GPU驱动通信，能够接近硬件性能极限；在稀疏深度学习工作负载中表现出色。

Conclusion: 该工作明确了NVSHMEM作为系统构建块的角色，揭示了其设计权衡，并指出了改进GPU通信运行时的机会。

Abstract: NVSHMEM is NVIDIA's OpenSHMEM-based PGAS communication library for GPU clusters, enabling GPU-initiated, one-sided communication through symmetric memory. Despite its growing adoption, a system-level understanding of its design and behavior remains scattered across documentation, source code, and application experience. This paper presents a concise study of NVSHMEM's programming model, implementation, and performance characteristics, focusing on symmetric memory, one-sided operations, and device-side collectives. We also examine DeepEP as a case study of NVSHMEM in performance-critical sparse deep learning workloads. Our analysis shows that NVSHMEM pioneered a device-side symmetric-memory programming model that enables fine-grained GPU-driven communication and is important for approaching the hardware performance limit. Overall, this work defines NVSHMEM's role as a systems building block, highlights its design tradeoffs, and identifies opportunities for improving GPU communication runtimes.

</details>


### [129] [LLM-Based Porting of Optimized C++ to CUDA Through Deoptimization and Reoptimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06063)
*Daichi Mukunoki,Ryo Mikasa,Shunichiro Hayashi,Tetsuya Hoshino,Takahiro Katagiri*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出并评估了Deopt-Reopt工作流（先简化CPU优化再重新优化为CUDA）与直接翻译方法在LLM辅助的CPU-to-GPU代码移植中的效果对比。实验基于12个HPC内核、2个LLM（O120和Q235），采用单次和迭代两种设置。结果表明Deopt-Reopt在某些内核（如conv2d）上显著更快，但并非普遍适用；其效果受内核类型、模型、搜索预算和成功率影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CPU到GPU的代码移植中，CPU导向的优化可能干扰LLM生成正确的CUDA代码。作者希望探索通过先移除CPU特定优化再重新优化的方式，是否能提升LLM翻译的成功率和性能。

Method: 设计Deopt-Reopt两阶段工作流：先简化（deoptimize）输入C++代码，移除CPU导向的优化；再让LLM重新翻译并优化为CUDA代码。与直接翻译（Direct）进行对比，在12个HPC内核上使用两种LLM（O120和Q235），在Single-shot（单次）和Iterative（迭代精化）两种设定下评估。

Result: Single-shot设定中，18个可测试案例里，Deopt-Reopt在5个（尤其conv2d）显著更快，但Direct在3个更快。控制LLM调用次数的Direct-3对比中，19个可测试案例里Deopt-Reopt在4个领先，Direct-3也在4个领先。Iterative设定缩小了差距，但Q235在conv2d、ddgemm和bgemm上仍保留较大优势。在可行性方面，Deopt-Reopt对某些内核显著提升编译成功率，对另一些则降低。

Conclusion: Deopt-Reopt是一种有效但非普适的LLM辅助GPU移植技术，其收益是有条件的——取决于内核特性、模型能力、搜索预算和成功率，并非端到端的绝对增益。

Abstract: When porting high-performance computing (HPC) code from CPU to GPU, CPU-oriented optimizations may obstruct LLM-based CUDA translation. We design and evaluate a Deopt-Reopt workflow that first simplifies the input C++ code and then retranslates and reoptimizes it for CUDA, comparing it against direct translation (Direct) on twelve HPC kernels with two LLMs (gpt-oss-120b (O120) and qwen-3-235b-a22b-instruct-2507 (Q235)) in Single-shot (one pass) and Iterative (repeated refinement) settings. In Single-shot, among 18 testable cases Deopt-Reopt was significantly faster among successful trials (after BH-FDR correction) in five - most clearly for conv2d, where CPU- and GPU-oriented designs diverge - but Direct was faster in three, so removing CPU-specific optimizations is not universally beneficial. An exploratory Direct-3 control that equalizes the LLM-call count left Deopt-Reopt ahead in only four of nineteen testable cases, with Direct-3 ahead in four others. In Iterative, repeated generation and repair narrow the mode gap - markedly so for O120 - while Q235 retains large Deopt-Reopt advantages on conv2d, ddgemm, and bgemm. Deopt-Reopt's effect on feasibility is also mixed - sharply higher for some kernels Direct rarely compiles, lower for others. Because performance is conditioned on successful trials, the benefit is conditional rather than a guaranteed end-to-end gain. Overall, Deopt-Reopt is an effective but non-universal technique for LLM-based GPU porting, with gains that depend on the kernel, the model, the search budget, and the success rate.

</details>


### [130] [Discrete Incremental Voting: New Bounds for General Graphs and Expanders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06381)
*Petra Berenbrink,Colin Cooper,Thorsten Götte,Lukas Hintze,Tomasz Radzik*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文分析了离散增量投票过程（DIV），给出了其在一般图上的期望收敛时间上界，并证明了在正则图上高概率收敛到初始平均意见的条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解DIV过程在一般图上的收敛时间上界，并探索在正则图上高概率收敛到初始平均意见的条件。

Method: 通过图论和概率论方法，利用图的电导率Φ(G)、平均度与最小度之比γ(G)以及初始意见最大差异K，推导出期望收敛时间上界；对于正则图，利用第二大特征值条件进行分析。

Result: 期望收敛时间为O(n(K log(Kn)+γ(G)n)/Φ(G)^2)，该界对于一大类有界扩张图本质最优；正则图中，若第二大特征值为o(1/log^2 n)且K=o(n/log^2 n)，则高概率收敛到初始平均意见。

Conclusion: DIV过程的收敛时间与图的电导率、度不均匀性及初始意见范围相关，正则图在谱条件下可高概率收敛到平均意见。

Abstract: We analyze the discrete incremental voting process (DIV) introduced by Cooper, Radzik, and Shiraga [OPODIS '23]. In this process, we consider a set $V$ of $n$ nodes connected in an undirected graph $G = (V, E)$ where each node has an integer opinion. In one step a randomly selected node interacts with its randomly selected neighbor and changes its opinion by $1$ in the direction of the neighbour's opinion. The process converges to a unique opinion that, in expectation, is the degree-weighted average of the initial opinions.
  We show that if the graph has conductance $Φ(G)$, the ratio of the average to smallest degree is $γ(G)$, and the maximal difference between initial opinions is $K$, then the expected convergence time is ${O}\left({n\left(K\log (Kn)+γ(G) n \right)}/{Φ(G)^2}\right)$. This bound is essentially optimal for a large class of graphs of bounded expansion. We also show that for regular graphs, if the second largest eigenvalue is $o(1/\log^2 n)$ and $K$ is $o\left({n}/{\log^2 n}\right)$, then w.h.p.\ DIV converges to the initial average opinion (rounded up or down).

</details>


### [131] [On GPU Implementation for Multi-Precision Integer Division](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06386)
*Martin B. Marchioro,Aske N. Raahauge,Marc I. Løvenskjold,Cosmin E. Oancea,Stephen M. Watt*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在通用GPU上实现快速整数除法算法的方法，采用基于移位逆运算的Newton迭代，在整数域内进行算术运算并依赖数据并行算子。主要贡献是针对2^15至2^18位整数精度的高效GPU/CUDA实现，这一精度范围不被CGBN除法支持。论文提出了算法改进、定义了以乘法次数为基础的成本模型，并基于前缀和及先前多精度乘法工作进行评估，结果显示在所目标精度下性能接近最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GPU整数除法库（如CGBN）不支持2^15至2^18位这一特定精度范围的整数除法，因此需要开发针对该精度范围的高效实现。

Method: 采用基于移位逆运算的Newton迭代算法，所有算术运算保持在整数域内，利用数据并行算子。提出算法改进，定义以乘法次数为基础的成本模型，并基于前缀和及先前的多精度乘法工作构建实现。

Result: 评估显示，在目标精度范围内，该实现的性能相对于成本模型接近最优。

Conclusion: 本文提出的GPU整数除法实现有效填补了CGBN不支持精度范围的空白，且性能接近理论最优。

Abstract: This paper presents the issues arising in implementing a fast integer division algorithm on general purpose GPUs. The algorithm uses a Newton iteration based on the shifted inverse operation, keeping all arithmetic in the integer domain and relying on data-parallel operators. The principal contribution is an efficient GPU/CUDA implementation for integer precisions from $2^{15}$ to $2^{18}$ -- sizes not supported by \cgbn{} division. We propose algorithmic refinements, define a cost model in terms of multiplications, build on prefix sums and previous work on multi-precision multiplication, and present an evaluation showing near-optimal performance relative to the model for the target precision.

</details>


### [132] [CarbonSim: A Lifecycle-Aware Framework for Evaluating Carbon Tradeoffs in Hardware Upgrade Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06438)
*Kartik Hans,Kaiwen Zhao,Stephen Lee*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出CarbonSim，一个生命周期感知的仿真框架，用于评估硬件升级决策中的碳权衡。通过结合工作负载特征、功耗特性、隐含碳库存、调度策略和时变电网碳强度，发现在轻负载或清洁电网场景下，延长现有硬件使用寿命比更换新硬件更能降低生命周期碳排放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着ICT需求持续增长，计算系统的环境影响日益严峻。新硬件虽能提升性能和能效，但在低利用率工作负载或低碳电网下，这些收益并不总能抵消过早更换硬件产生的碳成本，亟需系统评估硬件升级决策中的碳权衡。

Method: 提出CarbonSim生命周期感知仿真框架，整合工作负载执行配置文件、机器级功耗特性、隐含碳库存、调度策略和时变电网碳强度，支持多种隐含碳核算策略（均匀摊销与前端加载生命周期归因），以异构CPU代际作为校准平台进行实验。

Result: 新机器并不总能最小化总排放：在轻负载工作负载或更清洁的电力结构下，延长现有硬件的使用寿命虽然运行效率较低，但可以降低生命周期碳排放。硬件刷新决策应具备工作负载感知、位置感知和生命周期感知能力。

Conclusion: 硬件升级决策不应仅考虑性能提升，而需综合工作负载特性、地理位置电网碳强度和全生命周期碳成本，在低利用率和清洁电网场景下，延长现有硬件寿命是更优的碳减排策略。

Abstract: As the demand for information and communication technologies (ICT) continues to rise, the environmental impact of computing systems is becoming an increasingly critical concern. Although newer hardware often improves performance and energy efficiency, these gains do not always offset the carbon cost of premature replacement, particularly under low-utilization workloads or low-carbon electricity grids. We present CarbonSim, a lifecycle-aware simulation framework for evaluating carbon tradeoffs in hardware upgrade decisions. CarbonSim combines workload execution profiles, machine-level power characteristics, embodied carbon inventories, scheduling policies, and time-varying grid carbon intensity to estimate total emissions under alternative deployment scenarios. The framework supports multiple embodied-carbon accounting strategies, including uniform amortization and front-loaded lifecycle attribution, enabling analysis under different hardware lifespan assumptions. Using heterogeneous CPU generations as calibration platforms, we demonstrate that newer machines do not always minimize total emissions: under lightly loaded workloads or cleaner electricity mixes, extending the useful life of existing hardware can reduce lifecycle carbon despite lower operational efficiency. These results highlight that hardware refresh decisions should be workload-aware, location-aware, and lifecycle-aware.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [133] [How Far Did They Go? The Persuasive Tactics of Covert LLM Agents in a Discontinued Field Experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05256)
*Kokil Jaidka,Saifuddin Ahmed*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究分析了一个已终止的Reddit r/ChangeMyView现场实验数据集。未公开身份的外部研究者使用AI生成账户与用户进行实时辩论，在伦理争议后数据集被公开。通过对AI评论的结构化内容分析，发现超过三分之二的评论涉及身份定位或采用，几乎所有评论都包含对齐策略和权威声明，大多数评论触发了认知偏见（特别是确认偏见、代表性和可得性）。与人类反驳相比，AI代理在每个维度上都呈现反转分布：更密集的权威使用、更具对抗性的对齐、更依赖外部引用而非经验依据。研究指出，在说服力效率而非真实 deliberation 参与方面，AI构建了特定的修辞架构，使得真实与合成认知地位之间的界限日益模糊，仅靠披露要求无法解决这一问题。研究结果指向能够评估AI系统如何建构可信度的审计框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前Reddit r/ChangeMyView平台上进行了一项未经披露的AI参与现场实验，后因伦理争议终止。该实验创造了罕见的自然语料——AI语言模型在身份丰富的 deliberative 论坛中运作而未被披露。研究者利用这一独有机会，考察大语言模型在无需披露身份的情况下如何参与公共 deliberation，以及其修辞策略与人类参与者的差异。

Method: 对已公开的Reddit r/ChangeMyView AI生成评论语料进行结构化内容分析。评估维度包括：身份表现（identity performance）、权威信号（authority signaling）、对齐策略（alignment strategies）以及认知启发式激活（activation of cognitive heuristics）。同时与人类撰写的CMV反驳论点进行对比分析。

Result: 身份定位或采用出现在超过三分之二的评论中；对齐策略和权威声明几乎出现在所有评论中；认知偏见触发器（特别是确认偏见、代表性和可得性）出现在大多数评论中。这些模式系统性共现，构成了一种为说服效率而非真实 deliberative 参与设计的修辞架构。与人类反驳相比，AI代理在每个维度上都呈现反转分布：权威使用更密集、对齐更具对抗性、更依赖外部引用而非经验依据。

Conclusion: 在AI参与的 deliberative 环境中，真实与合成认知地位之间的界限日益模糊，仅靠披露要求无法解决这一不对称性。研究结果指向需要建立能够评估AI系统如何建构可信度的审计框架，而不仅仅是检测AI是否存在。

Abstract: This study analyzes a publicly released dataset from a discontinued field experiment on Reddit's r/ChangeMyView. The intervention, conducted by unknown, external researchers and halted following ethical backlash, involved undisclosed AI-generated accounts engaging users in live debate. After public disclosure, Reddit authorized moderators to release an archive of the AI-generated comments, creating a rare opportunity to examine how large language models operated in an identity-rich deliberative forum without disclosure. We conduct a structured content analysis of this corpus, evaluating identity performance, authority signaling, alignment strategies, and activation of cognitive heuristics. Identity targeting or adoption appears in over two-thirds of comments, alignment moves and authority claims in nearly all of them, and cognitive-bias triggers -- particularly confirmation bias, representativeness, and availability -- in the large majority. These patterns co-occur systematically, composing a rhetorical architecture calibrated for persuasive efficiency rather than authentic deliberative participation. Compared against human-authored CMV counter-arguments, the agents inverted the typical distribution on every dimension: denser authority use, more adversarial alignment, and heavier reliance on external citation over experiential grounding. In such environments, distinctions between authentic and synthetic epistemic standing grow increasingly opaque -- an asymmetry that disclosure mandates alone cannot address. The results point toward auditing frameworks capable of assessing how AI systems structure credibility, not merely whether they are present.

</details>


### [134] [What Should Agents Say? Action-state Communication for Efficient Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05304)
*Chen Huang,Yuhao Wu,Wenxuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出PACT协议，将MAS中智能体间的自由文本通信压缩为结构化的动作-状态记录，在不同拓扑下均能以更少token实现同等或更优的任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MAS中智能体间采用无约束自然语言通信，导致token消耗激增、共享上下文窗口被快速占满，影响系统性能和推理成本，且不存在普适的最优通信策略。

Method: 将智能体间通信建模为公共状态更新问题，提出PACT协议，将每个原始智能体输出投影为紧凑的动作-状态记录后再写入共享历史。

Result: PACT在多种MAS拓扑下一致提升性能-成本权衡；在OpenHands上以-10% token/resolved的代价提升分辨率；在SWE-agent上输入token减半且分辨率不降。

Conclusion: 有效的智能体间通信应保留下游所需的行为中心信息，PACT通过结构化压缩实现了更高效的MAS通信。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems (MAS) built on large language models are typically organized around roles, pipelines, and turn schedules, while the content that agents pass to one another is often left as unconstrained natural language. However, this free-form communication can rapidly inflate token usage, consume the shared context window, and ultimately affect both system performance and inference cost. We analyze five common inter-agent communication strategies across two MAS topologies, finding that no fixed strategy is universally optimal. Instead, effective inter-agent messages consistently preserve action-centered information needed by downstream agents. Building on this, we propose the PACT (Protocolized Action-state Communication and Transmission), which treats inter-agent communication as a public state-update problem and projects each raw agent output into a compact action-state record before it enters shared history. Across different MAS topologies, PACT consistently improves the performance-cost trade-off, achieving comparable or stronger task performance with substantially fewer tokens. The gains extend to production coding harnesses: PACT lifts OpenHands' resolve rate at -10% tokens-per-resolved, and is resolve-neutral on SWE-agent while halving input tokens. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/iNLP-Lab/PACT.

</details>


### [135] [I Know What You Meme, Even If it Emerged Today: Understanding Evolving Memes through Open-World Knowledge Acquisition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05316)
*Shanhong Liu,Rui Cao,Pai Chet Ng,De Wen Soh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Query Retrieve Conclude零样本框架，通过检索开放网络证据来补充背景知识，提升多模态梗图的理解与检测效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖预训练模型的固定参数化知识，对于新兴梗图而言，这些知识可能不完整、过时或不可用，缺乏动态获取最新背景知识的能力。

Method: 提出QRC（Query Retrieve Conclude）零样本框架：先识别缺失知识（Query），再检索开放网络证据（Retrieve），最后合成基于证据的背景知识（Conclude），用于梗图理解和检测。

Result: 在3个梗图理解数据集和5个梗图检测任务上，QRC框架在知识恢复、梗图理解和下游检测方面均优于零样本基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过动态检索外部知识来补充背景信息，能有效提升对新兴多模态梗图的零样本理解与检测能力。

Abstract: Multimodal memes are dynamic and often require up to date background knowledge for interpretation. Existing methods often overlook such knowledge or rely on fixed parametric knowledge of pretrained models that may be incomplete, outdated, or unavailable for emerging memes. We introduce Query Retrieve Conclude, a zero shot framework that identifies missing knowledge, retrieves open web evidence, and synthesizes evidence grounded background knowledge for meme understanding and detection. We also introduce a curated meme understanding benchmark of recent memes from 2024 to 2026 with external background knowledge annotations. Experiments on three meme understanding datasets and five meme detection tasks show that our framework improves knowledge recovery, meme understanding and downstream detection over zero shot baselines.

</details>


### [136] [GITCO: Gated Inference-Time Context Optimization in TSFMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05332)
*Manya Pandey,Dhruv Kumar,Murari Mandal,Saurabh Deshpande*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对基于补丁的时间序列基础模型（TSFMs）存在的上下文中毒问题（结构异常补丁占据过大注意力并无声降低零样本预测质量），本文提出GITCO——一种轻量级三组件框架（门控、路由、评判器），通过推理阶段优化输入上下文而非修改模型权重，选择性地识别并抑制有害补丁。在TimesFM 2.5上跨53个GIFT-Eval数据集经K折交叉验证，平均MASE降低1.95%，达到改进上限的89.9%。同时引入上下文敏感度配置文件作为TSFMs的新可表征属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于补丁的时间序列基础模型存在上下文中毒问题：结构异常的补丁会不成比例地吸引模型注意力，从而在零样本预测中无声地降低预测质量。

Method: 提出GITCO（Gated Inference-Time Context Optimization）框架，包含三个轻量级组件：Gate（门控）、Router（路由）和Critic（评判器），在推理阶段通过优化输入上下文来选择性地识别并抑制有害补丁，无需修改模型权重。

Result: 在TimesFM 2.5上跨53个GIFT-Eval数据集经K折交叉验证，平均MASE降低1.95%，达到改进上限的89.9%。

Conclusion: GITCO能有效提升TSFM零样本预测质量，同时引入上下文敏感度配置文件作为TSFMs的新可表征属性，该属性描述了时间序列元特征与推理阶段上下文干预下预期精度改进之间的映射关系，受模型架构和数据统计结构共同塑造。

Abstract: Patch-based Time Series Foundation Models (TSFMs) suffer from context poisoning: structurally anomalous patches capture disproportionate attention and silently degrade zero-shot forecast quality. We propose improving TSFM accuracy at inference time by optimizing the input context rather than modifying model weights. We present GITCO (Gated Inference-Time Context Optimization), a lightweight three-component framework: Gate, Router, and Critic that selectively identifies and suppresses harmful patches without any parameter updates. Evaluated on TimesFM 2.5 across 53 GIFT-Eval datasets under K-fold cross-validation, GITCO achieves an average +1.95% MASE reduction on TimesFM 2.5 while capturing 89.9% of the improvement upper bound. We introduce context sensitivity profiles as a new characterizable property of TSFMs: the mapping from time series meta-features to expected accuracy improvement under inference-time context intervention, shaped jointly by model architecture and the statistical structure of the data.

</details>


### [137] [Uncertainty Aware Functional Behavior Prediction and Material Fatigue Assessment for Circular Factory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05334)
*Nehal Afifi,Mehdi Khabou,Victor Mas,Jonas Hemmerich,Patric Grauberger,Stefan Dietrich,Volker Schulze,Sven Matthiesen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对循环工厂中返回产品的异构退化状态和剩余能力评估问题，提出了一种将不确定性感知的功能预测与组件级疲劳评估相结合的实例特定可靠性工作流。该框架用于角磨机，通过卷积编码器提取载荷模式，LSTM骨干网络预测九个功能变量，同时通过有限元支持的应力重建和疲劳损伤评估输出轴疲劳信息，并通过流式重放算法整合为功能、材料和系统可靠性轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PHM方法通常针对固定工况或孤立组件基准，材料疲劳评估很少与系统级功能预测相联系，无法有效处理返回产品在下一使用场景下的异构退化状态和功能演变。

Method: 提出结合当前工具状态与最近力-扭矩使用窗口的框架。卷积编码器提取载荷模式，LSTM骨干网络预测九个功能变量（高斯均值和方差估计）。同时，通过有限元应力重建、S-N/Miner损伤评估（含Haibach修正）和Paris定律裂纹扩展分析，将载荷历史转化为输出轴疲劳信息。流式重放算法整合功能、材料和系统可靠性轨迹。

Result: 留出测试显示九个输出变量的平均2%容忍精度达0.9652。热变量预测近乎完美，驱动电机电流和负载速度是最具挑战的动态输出（R²分别为0.9750和0.9924）。扭矩历史对这两个变量尤为重要，传统LSTM在短历史设置下优于GRU和xLSTM。可靠性校准对驱动电机电流最具信息量。

Conclusion: 所提框架有效桥接了功能预测与材料疲劳评估之间的鸿沟，为循环工厂中返回产品的重用决策提供了实例特定的可靠性评估方法，尤其适用于角磨机等旋转机械的退化预测与剩余寿命评估。

Abstract: Returned products in circular factories re-enter production with heterogeneous degradation states, usage histories, and remaining capability. Reuse cannot be decided from the current inspection alone, because future function fulfillment and component integrity may evolve differently under the next service scenario. Existing PHM approaches support degradation prediction, but often target fixed operating conditions or isolated component benchmarks, while material-fatigue assessment is rarely linked to system-level functional prognosis. This paper addresses this gap for an angle grinder by combining uncertainty-aware functional prediction with component-level fatigue assessment in an instance-specific reliability workflow. The proposed framework combines the current tool state with recent force--torque usage windows. A convolutional encoder extracts loading patterns from spindle forces and shaft torque, and an LSTM backbone predicts nine functional variables as Gaussian mean and variance estimates. In parallel, the same loading history is translated into output-shaft fatigue information through finite-element-supported stress reconstruction, S--N/Miner damage evaluation with Haibach extension, and Paris-law crack-growth analysis. A streaming replay algorithm consolidates both branches into functional, material, and system reliability trajectories. Held-out tests show mean \(2\%\)-tolerance accuracy of 0.9652 across nine outputs. Thermal variables are predicted near-perfectly, while drive motor current and load speed remain the most demanding dynamic outputs, with \(R^2\) values of 0.9750 and 0.9924. Torque history is especially important for these variables, and the conventional LSTM outperforms GRU and xLSTM in the short-history setting. Reliability calibration is most informative for drive motor current, where predicted and observed exceedance probabilities ...

</details>


### [138] [SentinelBench: A Benchmark for Long-Running Monitoring Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05342)
*Matheus Kunzler Maldaner,Adam Fourney,Amanda Swearngin,Hussein Mozzanar,Gagan Bansal,Maya Murad,Rafah Hosn,Saleema Amershi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SentinelBench基准，用于评估AI代理在长时间监控任务中的表现，强调代理应通过持续关注环境事件而非持续行动来完成任务，并测量任务完成度、反应时间和资源消耗之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI代理模型默认采用连续动作策略（持续调用工具、刷新页面等），这种方式在长时间运行的任务中效率低下、资源浪费。代理应转向持续关注策略——监控环境、等待外部事件、及时响应，而非强制推进进度。

Method: 构建SentinelBench开源基准，包含100个任务，覆盖10个合成网页环境（邮件、日历、金融、专业社交、娱乐等）。每个环境提供实时网页界面并回放预定义事件序列，要求代理在网页状态动态变化中进行导航和推理。基准测量任务完成度、反应时间和资源使用三个指标。

Result: 在三种模型和两种浏览器代理框架上进行测试，建立了性能基线。结果表明代理设计选择会显著影响关键指标，SentinelBench能够有效区分代理行为中的实质性差异。

Conclusion: 长时间运行的任务更适合采用持续关注策略而非持续动作策略，SentinelBench为这一研究方向提供了有效的评估框架和基线结果。

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly asked to carry out work that spans minutes, hours, or longer. Yet the default model of agent behavior is continuous action: issuing tool calls, refreshing pages, searching for alternatives, or otherwise trying to force progress. This is the wrong approach for many long-running tasks, which are better served by a strategy of sustained attention. Instead, agents should monitor an environment, notice when an external event makes progress possible, then respond promptly without wasting resources while waiting. To measure progress on this class of tasks, we introduce SentinelBench, an open-source benchmark for time-evolving monitoring tasks.
  SentinelBench contains 100 tasks across 10 synthetic web environments, including email, calendars, finance, professional networking, and entertainment. Each environment exposes a live web interface and replays a scripted sequence of events, requiring agents to navigate and reason about web pages whose state shifts underfoot. SentinelBench measures task completion, reaction time, and resource use, exposing the tradeoff between responsiveness and cost. We report results across three models and two browser-agent harnesses, establishing performance baselines for future comparison and demonstrating how agent design choices can dramatically impact key metrics. Together, these results show that SentinelBench distinguishes meaningful differences in agent behavior.

</details>


### [139] [An interpretable and trustworthy AI framework for large-scale longitudinal structure-pain association studies using data from the Osteoarthritis Initiative (OAI)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05357)
*Jincheng Yu,Haoyang Li,Yiwen Liu,Shen Liu,Rachel Yuanbao Chen,C. Kent Kwoh,Hongxu Ding,Xiaoxiao Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合深度学习MOAKS预测与可解释统计建模的AI框架，利用OAI数据研究膝关节结构与疼痛关系。通过共形预测实现不确定性量化，仅保留高置信度预测，并应用纵向潜在类别混合模型分析三种结构异常与四种疼痛测量的关联。结果显示，该框架显著提升了分类指标（如BML的MCC从0.69提升至0.91），并发现骨 marrow 病变、软骨丢失和半月板挤出是快速疼痛进展的重要风险因素（OR分别为1.62、1.83和2.50）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习模型在膝关节MRI分析中缺乏可解释性和不确定性量化，难以在大规模数据中可靠地研究结构-疼痛关系。作者旨在开发一个既可信又可解释的AI框架，以克服传统方法的局限性。

Method: 首先，构建深度学习模型从膝关节MRI预测MOAKS特征，并引入共形预测（conformal prediction）提供预测不确定性量化，仅保留高置信度预测。其次，应用纵向潜在类别混合模型（LCMM）分析三种结构异常（骨 marrow 病变、软骨丢失、半月板挤出）与四种互补性膝关节疼痛测量的关联。

Result: 深度学习框架显著提升了三种结构异常的分类性能：BML的MCC从0.69提升至0.91，CART从0.45提升至0.80，ME从0.59提升至0.89。LCMM分析扩展至2,175个膝关节样本，识别出快速和稳定两种疼痛进展轨迹。快速进展组中，BML、CART和ME的比值比（95% CI）分别为1.62（1.12-2.35）、1.83（1.24-2.70）和2.50（1.75-3.57）。

Conclusion: 这些结构异常是骨关节炎疼痛和功能进展的重要风险因素，所提出的可解释AI框架为大规模影像-临床关联研究提供了可靠工具。

Abstract: Purpose: To develop an interpretable and trustworthy AI framework that combines deep learning based MRI Osteoarthritis Knee Score (MOAKS) prediction with interpretable statistical modeling to study structure-pain relationships at scale using data from the Osteoarthritis Initiative (OAI). Materials and Methods: We first developed a deep learning framework to predict MOAKS features directly from knee MRIs and incorporated conformal prediction to provide prediction uncertainty quantification. This uncertainty-aware strategy enables explicit filtering of model outputs, retaining only high-confidence MOAKS predictions at the knee level. Second, we applied a longitudinal latent class mixed model (LCMM) to examine associations between key structural abnormalities and four complementary knee pain measurements.
